List of Dungeons & Dragons 3rd edition monsters

Copied from https://dungeonsdragons.fandom.com/wiki/List_of_Dungeons_%26_Dragons_3rd_edition_monsters

The following is a list of Dungeons & Dragons 3rd edition monsters. It includes all monsters appearing in an official Dungeons & Dragons 3.0 or 3.5 product.

---

Notes
Part of this list was sourced from the Monster Index, published on the Wizards of the Coast website on November 5, 2007. It lists 2,678 creatures appearing in first-party 3.0 and 3.5 sourcebooks. This list also includes 500 creatures appearing in the DragonDex list. It also includes creatures appearing in Dungeon and Polyhedron (with thanks to these lists), Living Greyhawk Journal, and the Diablo II D&D 3rd edition products.
The following canonical sources have yet to be to the table: Monster Manual (3.0) (2000), Wizards of the Coast web articles and web enhancements, Dragon Compendium Volume 1 (2005), Murder in Baldur's Gate (2013), and Legacy of the Crystal Shard (2014).
Monsters in the following 3.0 sourcebooks received an official D&D 3.5 update in the D&D v.3.5 Accessory Update Booklet: Manual of the Planes (3e) (2001), Monster Manual II (3e) (2002), Deities and Demigods (3e) (2002), Epic Level Handbook (2002), and Fiend Folio (3e) (2003). Additionally, Ghostwalk (2003) received an update in the Ghostwalk v.3.5 update. The remaining 3.0 books in the list, mostly adventure modules, received no official 3.5 update, although some monsters later appeared in 3.5 sourcebooks with revised stats. However, general conversion guidelines appear in the Accessory Update Booklet.
Deities and NPCs do not appear in this list. See also Category:Deities and Category:Characters.
For copyright reasons, game statistics for these monsters do not appear at this wiki.
